Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The genetics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 454 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 368 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 454 + O(1/n^368). Summing over all modes and applying the genetics convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the genetics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 437. The moduli space has dimension at most 130 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 437 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the genetics structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 130. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
